                                                                                                             respectively, the data showed.

                                                                        Singapore's imports of naphtha, reformates and other

                                                                    blendstocks rose 34% week over week to 239,733 mt in the week
                                                                    ended Oct. 7, the data showed.

                                                                        Russian inflows rebounded to 160,234 mt from none the
                                                                    previous week, and accounted for nearly 67% of the total arrivals.
                                                                    Shipments were last seen in the week of Sept. 23 at 147,557 mt.

                                                                        Imports from Southeast Asia also increased, with arrivals from
                                                                    Malaysia rising 25% week over week to 44,622 mt and volumes
                                                                    from Brunei nearly doubling to 9,980 mt.
